\varepsilon_{1}
and
\varepsilon_{2}
in
\mathbb{R}^{+}
such that

\displaystyle\int_{(S^{*})^{c}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|\mathrm{M}_{j,\ell}(\mathcal{L}_{(1)}/|\mathcal{T}|,\mathrm{i}\mathcal{T})a_{R}(g)\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g\lesssim_{\mathrm{M},\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}}\rho_{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}}(R,S)\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

for all particles
a_{R}
of the form
\mathcal{L}_{(1)}^{M}\mathcal{L}_{(2)}^{N}b_{R}
associated to
R\in\mathfrak{R}
, and all
S\in\mathfrak{R}
that contain
R
, where
M>\nu/2
and
N\geq 1
.
We may and shall assume that
R\subseteq T(o,1/2,h/2)
and
R^{*}=T(o,1,h)
, so
|R|\eqsim|R^{*}|\eqsim h\geq 1
, and that
S^{*}=T(o,r^{*},h^{*})
, where
r^{*}\geq 2\nu+1
and
h^{*}\geq(2\nu+1)^{2}h
.
In light of the discussion leading to (
7.6
), it will suffice to prove that

(7.10)

\displaystyle\int_{(S^{*})^{c}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g)\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g\lesssim_{\mathrm{M},\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}}\rho_{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}}(R,S)\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

To prove (
7.10
), we define four regions in
\mathbb{H}^{\nu}
using the Euclidean metric:

\displaystyle E_{1}

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t)\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z|\geq r^{*},|t|\geq 8\nu(h+|z|)\}

\displaystyle E_{2}

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t)\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:2\leq|z|\leq r^{*},|t|\geq h^{*}\}

\displaystyle E_{3}

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t)\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z|\geq r^{*},|t|\leq 8\nu(h+|z|)\}

\displaystyle E_{4}

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t)\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z|\leq 2,|t|\geq h^{*}\}.

Clearly
E_{1}\cup E_{2}\cup E_{3}\cup E_{4}\cup S^{*}=\mathbb{H}^{\nu}
.
The bulk of the proof is the estimation of the integral on the left-hand side of (
7.10
) on the four regions
E_{1}
,
E_{2}
,
E_{3}
and
E_{4}
.

We begin by estimating over
E_{1}
.
From the inclusion
\ell^{1}\subseteq\ell^{2}
, we see that

(7.11)

\displaystyle\int_{E_{1}}\biggr(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g)\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{1}}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}.

From the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the definition of
k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}
after (
7.6
), and Lemma
7.10
,

(7.12)

\displaystyle\int_{E_{1}}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{R^{*}}\int_{E_{1}}\bigl|k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|\left|c_{R}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\leq\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{1}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int_{E_{1}}w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime}).\left|k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\right)^{1/2}\left|c_{R}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{1}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}\left|c_{R}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g.

We will use several different versions of this calculation later.

Recall that
(z,t)^{-1}\cdot(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})=(z^{\prime}-z,t^{\prime}-t-S(z,z^{\prime}))
.
If
(z,t)\in R^{*}=T(0,1,h)
and
(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\in E_{1}
, then

(7.13)

|z+z^{\prime}|\geq|z^{\prime}|-|z|\geq r^{*}-1\qquad\text{and}\qquad|t^{\prime}-t-S(z,z^{\prime})|\geq|t^{\prime}|-|t|-|S(z,z^{\prime})|\geq h,

and so if
g\in R^{*}
and
g^{\prime}\in E_{1}
, then

g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in E_{1}^{*}:=\{(z^{\prime\prime},t^{\prime\prime})\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z^{\prime\prime}|\geq r^{*}-1,|t^{\prime\prime}|\geq h\},

whence

(7.14)

\displaystyle\int_{E_{1}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{E_{1}^{*}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{|z|>r^{*}-1}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{j+\ell}|z|^{2})^{-\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\,\mathrm{d}z\int_{|t|>h}2^{\ell}(1+2^{\ell}|t|)^{-1-\varepsilon}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\int_{|z|>2^{(j+\ell)/2}(r^{*}-1)}(1+|z|^{2})^{-\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\,\mathrm{d}z\int_{|t|>2^{\ell}h}(1+|t|)^{-1-\varepsilon}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\bigl(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*}\bigr)^{-\nu\varepsilon}\bigl(1+2^{\ell}h\bigr)^{-\varepsilon/2}.

From inequalities (
7.12
) to (
7.14
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{E_{1}}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}[\mathbbm{1}_{E_{1}^{*}}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}](g^{\prime})\bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}.

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}2^{m(j+\ell)}\bigl(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*}\bigr)^{-\nu\varepsilon}\bigl(1+2^{\ell}h\bigr)^{-\varepsilon/2}2^{n\ell}h^{n+1/2}\bigl\|a_{R}\bigr\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})};

We sum these inequalities as indicated by (
7.11
), taking
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell\leq 0
and
0
otherwise, so that
2^{m(j+\ell)}=\min\{1,2^{M(j+\ell)}\}
, and taking
n
to be
N
if
2^{\ell}h\leq 1
and
0
otherwise, so that
2^{n\ell}=\min\{1,2^{N\ell}\}
.
Then

(7.15)

\displaystyle\int_{E_{1}}\biggr(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g)\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{1}}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon,M,N}\Bigl(\frac{1}{r^{*}}\Bigr)^{2M\nu\varepsilon/(2M+\nu\varepsilon)}\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

this is an estimate of the required form.
We estimated the sum in the above expression as follows: define

\begin{gathered}J_{1}:=\{j:2^{j+\ell}<1/r^{*}\},\qquad J_{2}:=\{j:1/r^{*}\leq 2^{j+\ell}<1\}\qquad J_{3}:=\{j:2^{j+\ell}\geq 1\},\\
L_{1}:=\{\ell:2^{\ell}h<1\}\qquad L_{2}:=\{\ell:2^{\ell}h\geq 1\};\end{gathered}

then

(7.16)

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\min\{1,2^{M(j+\ell)}\}(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}\}

\displaystyle\eqsim\left(\sum_{j\in J_{1}}2^{M(j+\ell)}+\sum_{j\in J_{2}}2^{M(j+\ell)}(2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}+\sum_{j\in J_{3}}(2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\eqsim_{M,\varepsilon}\Bigl((r^{*})^{-2M}+(r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}+(r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}\Bigr),

whence

(7.17)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,\ell}\min\{1,2^{M(j+\ell)}\}(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}(1+2^{\ell}h)^{-\varepsilon/2}\min\{1,(2^{\ell}h)^{N}\}

\displaystyle\eqsim_{M,\varepsilon}(r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{\ell\in L_{1}}(2^{\ell}h)^{N}+\sum_{\ell\in L_{2}}(2^{\ell}h)^{-\varepsilon/2}\right)

\displaystyle\eqsim_{N,\varepsilon}\Bigl(\frac{1}{r^{*}}\Bigr)^{\nu\varepsilon},

The next step of the proof is to estimate the integral over
E_{2}
.
The argument is very similar to that used to treat the region
E_{1}
.
Indeed, apart from replacing
E_{1}
by
E_{2}
, we replace the argument for (
7.13
) and (
7.14
) by the observation that if
(z,t)\in R^{*}=T(0,1,h)
and
(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\in E_{2}
, then

|z^{\prime}-z|\geq|z^{\prime}|-|z|\geq 1\qquad\text{and}\qquad|t^{\prime}-t-S(z,z^{\prime})|\geq|t^{\prime}|-|t|-|S(z,z^{\prime})|\geq h^{*}/4,

so if
g\in R^{*}
and
g^{\prime}\in E_{2}
, then

g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in E_{2}^{*}:=\{(z^{\prime\prime},t^{\prime\prime})\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z^{\prime\prime}|\geq 1,|t^{\prime\prime}|\geq h^{*}/4\},

whence

\displaystyle\int_{E_{2}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{E_{2}^{*}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{|z|>1}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{j+\ell}|z|^{2})^{-\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\,\mathrm{d}z\int_{|t|>h^{*}/4}2^{\ell}(1+2^{\ell}|t|)^{-1-\varepsilon}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\bigl(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}\bigr)^{-2\nu\varepsilon}\bigl(1+2^{\ell}h^{*}\bigr)^{-\varepsilon}.

By making these modifications and summing much as before, we conclude that

\int_{E_{2}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\lesssim\Bigl(\frac{h}{h^{*}}\Bigr)^{\varepsilon}\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})},

which is again of the required form.

Now we estimate the integral over
E_{3}
.
We decompose this region into dyadic pieces
E_{3,k}
, given by

\displaystyle E_{3,k}

\displaystyle:=\{(z,u):z\in A_{k},|u|\leq 8\nu(h+|z|)\}.

where

\displaystyle A_{k}

\displaystyle:=\{z\in\mathbb{C}^{\nu}:2^{k}r^{*}<|z|\leq 2^{k+1}r^{*}\}.

For later purposes, we define
A_{k}^{*}:=\{z\in\mathbb{C}^{\nu}:2^{k}r^{*}-1<|z|\leq 2^{k+1}r^{*}+1\}
.

We take a smooth compactly supported real-valued function
\Phi
on
\mathbb{R}\setminus\{0\}
such that
\Phi\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{H}
, so that
\mathrm{H}(2^{\ell}i\mathcal{T})=\Phi(2^{\ell}i\mathcal{T})\mathrm{H}(2^{\ell}i\mathcal{T})
, and define

(7.18)

c_{R,\ell}:=\Phi(2^{\ell}i\mathcal{T})c_{R};

we do not make explicit the dependence of
c_{R,\ell}
on choices of
m
and
n
.
From spectral theory,

(7.19)

\biggl(\sum_{\ell}\left\|c_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\lesssim_{\Phi}\left\|c_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

The operator
\Phi(2^{\ell}i\mathcal{T})
is a convolution in the central variable with a Schwartz function
\varphi_{\ell}
, say, so
c_{R,\ell}=c_{R}\ast_{(2)}\varphi_{\ell}
, and so
c_{R,\ell}
is supported in
\{(z,t):|z|\leq 1\}
.
Now, from (
7.6
),

\displaystyle\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}.

\displaystyle\qquad=\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|c_{R,\ell}\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{G}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{C(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=:\mathrm{I}^{G}_{k}+\mathrm{I}^{C}_{k}+\mathrm{I}^{D}_{k},

say, where we have split the region
\{(z,t):|z|\leq 1,t\in\mathbb{R}\}
in which
c_{R,\ell}
is supported into two subsets,
G
and
B
, and then split
B
into two subsets which depend on
g^{\prime}
, as follows:

(7.20)

\displaystyle G

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t):|z|\leq 1,|t|\geq 2h\}

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle:=\{(z,t):|z|\leq 1,|t|<2h\}

\displaystyle C(g^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\{g\in B:P_{\mathbb{R}}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\notin[-2h,2h]\}

\displaystyle D(g^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\{g\in B:P_{\mathbb{R}}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\in[-2h,2h]\}.

Here
P_{\mathbb{R}}
denotes the (noncanonical) projection of
\mathbb{H}^{\nu}
onto
\mathbb{R}
given by
(z,t)\mapsto t
.

First we consider the terms
\mathrm{I}^{G}_{k}
.
From the inclusion
\ell^{1}\subseteq\ell^{2}
, and a calculation like (
7.12
),

(7.21)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{G}_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{3,k}}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{G}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}\int_{E_{3,k}}\bigl|k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|\left|\mathbbm{1}_{G}c_{R,\ell}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}\sum_{j,\ell}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}\biggl(\int_{E_{3,k}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{G}c_{R,\ell}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g.

When
g\in G
and
g^{\prime}\in E_{3,k}
,
g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in P^{-1}A^{*}_{k}
, so, much as argued from (
7.13
) to (
7.14
),

\displaystyle\biggl(\int_{E_{3,k}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\int_{P^{-1}{A}_{k}^{*}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}.

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl(1+2^{(j+\ell+2k)/2}r^{*}\bigr)^{-\nu\varepsilon}\qquad\forall g\in G.

Since
\varphi
is a Schwartz function, much as shown in Lemma
7.7
, for all
S\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\int_{G}\left|c_{R,\ell}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus[-2h,2h]}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{[-h,h]}\left|c_{R}(z,t^{\prime})\right|\left|\varphi_{\ell}(t-t^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus[-h,h]}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{[-h,h]}\left|c_{R}(z,t^{\prime})\right|\left|\varphi_{\ell}(t^{\prime\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus[-2^{\ell}h,2^{\ell}h]}\left|\varphi(t^{\prime\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime\prime}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{[-h,h]}\left|c_{R}(z,t^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varphi}(1+2^{\ell}h)^{-S}\left\|c_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{1}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

\displaystyle\leq(1+2^{\ell}h)^{-S}h^{N}h^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

by Lemma
2.2
.
To conclude, by taking
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell\leq 0
and
0
otherwise and
n
to be
N
if
2^{\ell}h\leq 1
and
0
otherwise, and summing as in (
7.16
) and (
7.17
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\mathrm{I}^{G}_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}(2^{k}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}h^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\Bigl(\frac{1}{r^{*}}\Bigr)^{\nu\varepsilon}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

Next we estimate the terms
\mathrm{I}^{C}_{k}
, given by

\mathrm{I}^{C}_{k}=\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{C(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}.

The argument used to prove (
7.21
) shows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{C}_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}\sum_{j,\ell}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}\int_{G}\biggl(\int_{E_{3,k}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}\left|\mathbbm{1}_{C(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}(g)\right|\,\mathrm{d}g.

Let
E_{3,k}^{*}
be the set
\{(z,t)\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:z\in A_{k}^{*},|t|\geq 2h\}
.
By the definition of
C(g^{\prime})
(see (
7.20
)), if
g\in G
and
g^{\prime}\in E_{3,k}
, then
g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in E^{*}_{3,k}
, so

\displaystyle\biggl(\int_{E_{3,k}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl(\int_{E^{*}_{3,k}}\frac{1}{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+2^{(j+\ell)/2}2^{k}r^{*})^{-\nu\varepsilon}(1+2^{\ell}h)^{-\varepsilon/2}

for all
g\in G
.
Once again, we choose
m
to be
M
when
j+\ell
is small and
0
otherwise, and
n
to be
N
when
\ell
is small and
0
otherwise, and sum.
This leads to the conclusion that

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{C}_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigl(\frac{1}{2^{k}r^{*}}\Bigr)^{\nu\varepsilon}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})},

which in turn gives the desired estimate

\int_{E_{3}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{C(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\lesssim\Bigl(\frac{1}{r^{*}}\Bigr)^{\nu\varepsilon}\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

Finally, we deal with the terms
\mathrm{I}^{D}_{k}
, given by

\mathrm{I}^{D}_{k}=\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,n}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}.

We take
n=0
throughout this estimate, and assume for the moment that
r^{*}\geq h
.

The projection
PD(g^{\prime})
of
D(g^{\prime})
is a subset of
\mathbb{C}^{\nu}
, and we claim that

(7.22)

\displaystyle|PD(g^{\prime})|\lesssim\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}.

To see this, we fix
(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\in E_{3,k}
, and take
(z,t)\in D(g^{\prime})
.
Then
|z|_{\infty}\leq 1
and
|t|\leq 2h
since
(z,t)\in B
, and further
|t^{\prime}-t-S(z^{\prime},z)|\leq 2h
, that is,

-4h\leq t-2h\leq S(z^{\prime},z)-t^{\prime}\leq t+2h\leq 4h
.
Now
S(z^{\prime},z)
is a euclidean inner product
Jz^{\prime}\cdot z
, where the euclidean norm of
Jz^{\prime}
is
8\nu
multiplied by the euclidean norm of
z^{\prime}
, which is bounded below by a multiple of
2^{k}r^{*}
.
So these inequalities describe a slice of
\mathbb{C}^{\nu}
of thickness of the order of
h/2^{k}r^{*}
, and the measure of the intersection of this slice with
\{z\in\mathbb{C}^{\nu}:|z|_{\infty}\leq 1\}
is as claimed.

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

(7.23)

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}^{D}_{k}\leq\left|E_{3,k}\right|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{3,k}}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}.

From the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, (
7.22
), and changes of variables and order of integration,

\displaystyle\int_{E_{3,k}}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\int_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}](g)\right|\,\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=\iint_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|\,\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\right|\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}z\biggr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\iint_{E_{3,k}}\biggl(\int_{PD(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})))\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}z\biggr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|PD(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\right|\biggl(\int_{PD(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z\biggr)\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{B}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{B}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}((-z,-t)(z^{\prime},t^{\prime}))}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{B}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t^{\prime}-t-S(z,z^{\prime}))}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{B}c_{R,\ell}](z,t)\right|^{2}}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}.

Now when
(z,t)\in B
and
(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\in E_{3,k}
,
\left|z-z^{\prime}\right|\geq\left|z^{\prime}\right|-\left|z\right|\gtrsim 2^{k}r^{*}
, and so

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{j+\ell}|z^{\prime}-z|^{2})^{-\nu(1+\varepsilon)}2^{\ell}(1+2^{\ell}|t^{\prime}|)^{-1-\varepsilon}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{k+(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}.

Hence by further changes of order of integration and of variables, and Lemma
7.10
,

\displaystyle\int_{E_{3,k}}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{A_{k}}\Bigl(2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{k+(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|c_{R,\ell}(z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\,\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(z^{\prime}-z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\Bigr)\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{k+(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|c_{R,\ell}(z,t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{A_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},t)\,\left|k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(z^{\prime},t)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{k+(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}2^{2m(j+\ell)}\left\|c_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}.

We sum this estimate over
j
and
\ell
, much as in (
7.16
) and (
7.17
), using (
7.18
) and (
7.19
) and Lemma
2.2
, and taking
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell\leq 0
and
0
otherwise and
n
to be
0
.
We recall that
c_{R,\ell}
also depends on
m
, and hence on
j
and
\ell
, and here write
c^{m}_{R,\ell}
instead of
c_{R,\ell}
.
Then, assuming that
2M>\varepsilon
, we see that

\displaystyle\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{3,k}}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c^{m}_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}\sum_{j,\ell}\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}2^{\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{k+(j+\ell)/2}r^{*})^{-2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}\min\{1,2^{2M(j+\ell)}\}\left\|c^{m}_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}

\displaystyle\eqsim_{M,\varepsilon}\frac{h}{2^{k}r^{*}}\sum_{\ell}\left(\frac{1}{(2^{k}r^{*})^{4M+2\nu}}+\frac{1}{(2^{k}r^{*})^{2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}}+\frac{1}{(2^{k}r^{*})^{2\nu(1+\varepsilon)}}\right)\left\|c^{m}_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{M,\varepsilon}\frac{h}{2^{2k\nu(1+\varepsilon)+k}(r^{*})^{2\nu(1+\varepsilon)+1}}\sum_{\ell}\left(\left\|c^{M}_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}+\left\|c^{0}_{R,\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\Phi,M}\frac{h}{2^{2k\nu(1+\varepsilon)+k}(r^{*})^{2\nu(1+\varepsilon)+1}}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}.

By combining this last inequality with (
7.23
) and the estimate
\left|E_{3,k}\right|\lesssim(2^{k}r^{*})^{2\nu+1}
(which holds since
h\leq r^{*}
), we see that

(7.24)

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\mathrm{I}^{D}_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\left|E_{3,k}\right|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{3,k}}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{D(g^{\prime})}c_{R,\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k^{m,0}_{j,\ell}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M},\Phi,\varepsilon}\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}(2^{k}r^{*})^{\nu+1/2}\frac{h^{1/2}}{2^{k\nu(1+\varepsilon)+k/2}(r^{*})^{\nu+\nu\varepsilon+1/2}}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

\displaystyle\eqsim_{\varepsilon}\Bigl(\frac{1}{r^{*}}\Bigr)^{\nu\varepsilon/2}\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

Thus the required estimate for the integral over
E_{3}
is proved when
r^{*}\geq h
.

If
r^{*}\geq h
, we modify the argument above slightly.
We use the inclusion
D(g^{\prime})\subseteq B
and replace
PD(g^{\prime})
by
PB
in the first stage of our calculations.
The expression
(h\left|E_{3,k}\right|/2^{k}r^{*})^{1/2}
that appears in (
7.24
) is then replaced by
\left|E_{3,k}\right|^{1/2}
, and with our alternative assumption,
\left|E_{3,k}\right|^{1/2}\lesssim(2^{k}r^{*})^{2\nu}h
, which leads us to the same conclusion.

Finally, we estimate the integral over
E_{4}
.
If
\mathrm{H}(\lambda/\mu)\neq 0
and
\mathrm{H}(\mu)\neq 0
, then
\mu,\lambda/\mu\in(1/2,2)
and so
\lambda\in(-1/4,4)
.
Hence there is a smooth, compactly supported function
\Phi
on
\mathbb{R}^{+}
such that

\Phi(\lambda)=\Phi(\lambda)\,\mathrm{H}(\lambda/\mu)\,\mathrm{H}(\mu)\qquad\forall\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{R}^{+},

whence

\Phi(2^{-j-\ell}\lambda)=\Phi(2^{-j-\ell}\lambda)\,\mathrm{H}(2^{-j}\lambda/\mu)\,\mathrm{H}(2^{-\ell}\mu)\qquad\forall\lambda,\mu\in\mathbb{R}^{+}.

Taking
\mu
to be
1
shows that

\Phi(2^{-j}\lambda)=\Phi(2^{-j}\lambda)\,\mathrm{H}(2^{-j}\lambda)\qquad\forall\lambda\in\mathbb{R}^{+}.

Then
\mathrm{M}_{j,\ell}(\mathcal{L}_{(1)}/|\mathcal{T}|,\mathrm{i}\mathcal{T})\Phi(2^{-j-\ell}\mathcal{L}_{(1)})=\mathrm{M}_{j,\ell}(\mathcal{L}_{(1)}/|\mathcal{T}|,\mathrm{i}\mathcal{T})
.
We define

c_{R,j+\ell}:=\Phi(2^{-j-\ell}\mathcal{L}_{(1)})c_{R},

keeping implicit the dependence of
c_{R,j+\ell}
on
m
and
n
.
From spectral theory,

\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left\|c_{R,j+\ell}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\lesssim_{\Phi}\left\|c_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

We divide the region
E_{4}
into dyadic pieces:

E_{4}=\bigcup_{k\in\mathbb{N}}E_{4,k}:=\bigcup_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\{(z,t):|z|\leq 2,2^{k}h^{*}<|t|\leq 2^{k+1}h^{*}\},

and define sets

\displaystyle A_{{k}}:=\{g\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:d_{(1)}(g,R)\geq 2^{k}h^{*}\}\qquad\text{and}\qquad B_{{k}}:=(A_{{k}})^{c}\setminus R^{*}.

Then
\mathbb{H}^{\nu}=A_{{k}}\cup B_{{k}}\cup R^{*}
.
Much as in the estimate over
E_{3}
, we see that

\displaystyle\int_{E_{4}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|c_{R}\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\int_{E_{4,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{A_{{k}}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\int_{E_{4,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{B_{{k}}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\int_{E_{4,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\Bigl|[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{A}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{B}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{R},

say.

To treat the term
\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{A}
, we observe that convolution with
k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}
is bounded on
\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})
independently of
j
,
\ell
,
m
and
n
by spectral theory, so that taking
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell\leq 0
and
0
otherwise and
n
to be
N
if
2^{\ell}h\leq 1
and
0
otherwise shows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{A}

\displaystyle\leq\left|E_{4,k}\right|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{4,k}}\Bigl|\mathbbm{1}_{A_{{k}}}c_{R,j+\ell}\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M},M,N}\left|E_{4,k}\right|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\min\{1,2^{j+\ell}\}^{M}\min\{1,2^{\ell}h\}^{N}\int_{A_{{k}}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}.

Since
d_{(1)}(A_{{k}},R)\geq(2^{k}h^{*})^{1/2}
, it follows from Lemma
7.7
that for all
S\in(\delta,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\int_{A_{{k}}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle=\int_{A_{{k}}}\left|\Phi(2^{-j-\ell}\mathcal{L}_{(1)})a_{R}(g)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\Phi,S}(1+2^{j+\ell+k}h^{*})^{-S}\}\left|R\right|\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}.

We recall that
j
is nonnegative, combine the last two inequalities, and sum over
k
, much as above.
Assuming that
S>M>N\geq 1
, we see that
\sum_{k}\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{A}
is dominated by a multiple of

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\left|E_{4,k}\right|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\frac{\min\{1,2^{j+\ell}\}^{M}\min\{1,2^{\ell}h\}^{N}}{(1+2^{j+\ell+k}h^{*})^{S}}\biggr)^{1/2}\left|R\right|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\Bigl(\frac{h}{h^{*}}\Bigr)^{(M-1)/2}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

To treat
\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{B}
, we note that, if
g^{\prime}=(z^{\prime},t^{\prime})\in E_{4,k}
and
g=(z,t)\in(A_{{k}})^{c}
, then

|t^{\prime}-t-S(z^{\prime},z)|\geq 2^{k}h^{*}-(2^{k-2}h^{*})^{1/2}-16\nu(2^{k-2}h^{*})^{1/2}\geq 2^{k-2}h^{*},

whence

g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in E_{4,k}^{*}:=\{(z^{\prime\prime},t^{\prime\prime})\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|t^{\prime\prime}|>2^{k-2}h^{*}\}.

Now

\displaystyle\int_{E^{*}_{4,k}}\frac{1}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{C}^{\nu}}\int_{|t^{\prime\prime}|>2^{k-2}h^{*}}\frac{2^{(j+\ell)\nu}2^{\ell}}{(1+2^{j+\ell}|z^{\prime\prime}|^{2})^{\nu(1+\varepsilon)}(1+2^{\ell}|t^{\prime\prime}|)^{1+\varepsilon}}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon}(1+2^{k}2^{\ell}h^{*})^{-\varepsilon}.

From this inequality, Lemma
7.10
, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Lemma
7.7
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{B}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{E_{4,k}}\Bigl|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{{k}}}c_{R,j+\ell}\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{B_{{k}}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|\int_{E_{4,k}}\Bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j,\ell}\int_{B_{{k}}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|\int_{E_{4,k}^{*}}\Bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\Bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathrm{M}}\sum_{j,\ell}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}\int_{B_{{k}}}\bigl|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g\Bigl(\int_{E^{*}_{4,k}}\frac{1}{w_{j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\Bigr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\sum_{j,\ell}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}(1+2^{k}2^{\ell}h^{*})^{-\varepsilon/2}\int_{B_{{k}}}\bigl|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\bigr|\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\Phi}\sum_{j,\ell}2^{m(j+\ell)+n\ell}(1+2^{k}2^{\ell}h^{*})^{-\varepsilon/2}(1+2^{j+\ell})^{-\varepsilon}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

We take
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell\leq 0
and
0
otherwise and
n
to be
N
if
2^{\ell}h\leq 1
and
0
otherwise, and sum much as in (
7.16
) and (
7.17
).
Then

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{B}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-k\varepsilon/2}\left(\frac{h}{h^{*}}\right)^{\varepsilon/2}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

To treat the term
\mathrm{I}_{4,k}^{R}
, we first observe that if
g\in R^{*}
and
g^{\prime}\in E_{4,k}
, then

g^{-1}g^{\prime}\in E_{4,k}^{*}:=\{(z^{\prime\prime},u^{\prime\prime})\in\mathbb{H}^{\nu}:|z^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3,(2^{k}-16\nu)h^{*}<|t|<(2^{k+1}+16\nu)h^{*}\}.

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\int_{E_{4,k}}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left|[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle=|E_{4,k}|^{1/2}\biggl(\sum_{j,\ell}\left\|[[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}]\mathbbm{1}_{E_{4,k}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}\biggr)^{1/2}.

We set
w_{1,j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(g):=2^{-\nu(j+\ell)}(1+2^{j+\ell}|z|^{2})^{\nu(1+\varepsilon)}
and
w_{2,j,\ell}^{\varepsilon}(g):=2^{-\ell}(1+2^{\ell}|t|)^{1+\varepsilon}
.
For all
f\in L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})
,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}[[[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}]\mathbbm{1}_{E_{4,k}}](g^{\prime})\,f(g^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\int_{R^{*}}\int_{E_{4,k}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|\bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}g

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(\int_{R^{*}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g\right)^{1/2}\biggl(\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\biggl(\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\biggl(\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|^{2}\frac{w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}{w^{\varepsilon}_{2,j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\int_{E_{4,k}}\frac{\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}}{w^{\varepsilon}_{1,j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\biggl(\int_{R^{*}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\frac{2^{\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}\bigl|k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\bigr|^{2}w^{\varepsilon}_{j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\int_{E_{4,k}}\frac{\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}}{w^{\varepsilon}_{1,j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\biggl(\int_{R^{*}}\frac{2^{2m(j+\ell)+(2n+1)\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\frac{\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}}{w^{\varepsilon}_{1,j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)\,\mathrm{d}g\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{2^{m(j+\ell)+(n+1/2)\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\int_{R^{*}}\frac{\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}}{w^{\varepsilon}_{1,j,\ell}(g^{-1}g^{\prime})}\,\mathrm{d}g\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\frac{2^{m(j+\ell)+(n+1/2)\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}h^{1/2}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}.

By combining the last two inequalities, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{H}^{\nu}}[[[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}]\mathbbm{1}_{E_{4,k}}](g^{\prime})\,f(g^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim_{\varepsilon}\frac{2^{m(j+\ell)+(n+1/2)\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}h^{1/2}\left(\int_{R^{*}}\left|c_{R,j+\ell}(g)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g\right)^{1/2}\biggl(\int_{E_{4,k}}\left|f(g^{\prime})\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}g^{\prime}\biggr)^{1/2}

for all
f\in L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})
.
By the converse of the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

\left\|[[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}]\mathbbm{1}_{E_{4,k}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}\lesssim\frac{2^{m(j+\ell)+(n+1/2)\ell}}{(1+2^{\ell}2^{k}h^{*})^{1+\varepsilon}}h^{1/2}\left\|[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}.

We take
m
to be
M
if
j+\ell<0
and
0
otherwise, and
n
to be
1
if
\ell<0
and
0
otherwise.
Summing as before, it is now straightforward to see that

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}}|E_{4,k}|^{1/2}\left(\sum_{j,\ell}\left\|[[\mathbbm{1}_{R^{*}}c_{R,j+\ell}]\ast_{(1)}k_{j,\ell}^{m,n}]\mathbbm{1}_{E_{4,k}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{h}{h^{*}}\right)^{\varepsilon/2}|R|^{1/2}\left\|a_{R}\right\|_{\mathsf{L}^{2}(\mathbb{H}^{\nu})}

as required, and the proof of Theorem E is complete.
∎
